Tracks every PayMongo top-up source for de-duplication/recovery — exactly one of `commuter_id`/`driver_id` is set per row (mirrors `reservations.trip_id`/`uv_session_id`/`uv_slot_id`'s existing "exactly one of several nullable FKs" convention). `method` records the commuter/driver's actual chosen demo button (`gcash` or `paymaya`) even though the real outbound PayMongo API call is always typed `gcash` — see Core Flow 15a.

### `association_applications` (legacy, unused by current code)
`id, association_id, driver_id, vehicle_id, status (pending|approved|rejected), created_at` — superseded by `operator_association_applications`; drivers/vehicles no longer apply to an association directly (see Org Hierarchy flow).

### `association_memberships` (legacy, unused by current code)
`id, association_id, driver_id, vehicle_id, joined_at` — superseded by `operators.association_id` (transitive membership).

### `qr_boards`
`id, commuter_id, driver_id, token, status (pending|used), created_at`

### `rebook_requests` (added migration 035, widened migration 047)
`id, reservation_id (FK→reservations), commuter_id (FK→commuters), reviewed_by_operator_id (FK→operators, nullable, migration 047), status (pending|approved|rejected|fulfilled|expired, default pending — widened migration 047 from the original pending|fulfilled|dismissed; 'dismissed' renamed to 'rejected'), requested_at, reviewed_at (nullable, migration 047), rejection_reason (nullable, migration 047), resolved_at` — `UNIQUE (reservation_id, status)` (blocks a duplicate *pending* request for the same booking, but allows a fresh one later once an earlier one leaves `pending`/`approved`). A commuter-initiated flag asking for a rebook — this app has no push/in-app notification system, so the only way it can reach the operator is as a visible badge + Approve/Reject on the operator's own trip/slot manifest (`bus_trips/get_trip_bookings.php`/`uv_slots/get_slot_bookings.php`). `approved` grants the commuter permission to pick their own new trip (see Core Flow 9.4's 2-step rewrite) — the request itself never creates a reservation; `reservations/complete_rebook.php` sets `status='fulfilled'` once the commuter actually completes it. `expired` is set opportunistically (`config/rebook_eligibility.php`'s `expireStaleRebookRequests()`, no cron) for a `pending`/`approved` row whose underlying booking's eligibility window has lapsed. See Core Flow 9a point 8.

### `ltfrb_fare_guides` (added migration 036)
`id, vehicle_type (Jeepney|UV Express|Bus), label, file_path, sort_order, created_at` — no owner column, global system reference data. Seeded once directly by the migration's own `INSERT`s (1 Jeepney, 1 UV Express, 7 Bus — LTFRB has no single canonical Bus fare guide, City/Provincial × 5 class tiers each publish separately). No POST/PUT/DELETE endpoint exists for this table anywhere in the API — read-only by construction. See Core Flow 13a.

### `terminals`
`id, operator_id, name, latitude, longitude, created_at`

## Key Relationships
- `drivers.operator_id` → nullable in the schema, but always set at creation in practice (`drivers/drivers.php` POST requires it). `drivers.association_id` is legacy/unused — a driver's association is derived transitively via `operator_id → operators.association_id`, not stored per-driver.
- `wallets` is 1:1 with `commuters`, auto-created on commuter registration
- `wallet_transactions` is polymorphic on `(owner_type, owner_id)` — covers both commuters and drivers
- `routes.waypoints` is a JSON column, not a separate table (despite `route_waypoints` table existing)
- A driver can only have one active `route_assignment`
- **Note:** this schema list predates the live Bus/UV booking system (`reservations`, `trips`, `uv_sessions`, `uv_slots`, `fare_matrix`, `seats`, `quota_submissions`, etc.) — see `transync-backend/migrations/003_booking_redesign.sql` onward and the Booking/Org-Hierarchy/Remittance Core Flow notes above for that schema; it hasn't been backfilled here. Tables added by migrations 009–016 (all documented in the Core Flow sections above, not repeated here): `operator_association_applications`, `remittance_rates`, `jeepney_quota_tiers`, `withdrawal_requests`, `bus_service_types`. `driver_operator_applications` (migration 011) was later dropped (migration 017) when driver self-registration was reverted. Migration 018: moved `bus_service_types` from operator- to association-owned (`association_id` added, `operator_id` now nullable/legacy), added `bus_layout_templates`, and (since reverted, see migration 019) briefly added a `remittance_submissions` table + `reservations.remittance_status`/`remittance_submission_id` columns for a submit/review remittance flow that turned out to be the wrong design. Migration 019 (Core Flow 9/12/15): dropped `remittance_submissions` and those two reservation columns entirely, added `late_cancelled` to `reservations.cancellation_type`, added `subscription_rates` and `subscription_payments`. Columns added to existing tables: `reservations.payment_status/cancellation_type/rebooked_from_reservation_id/rebooked_to_reservation_id/system_fee_amount/operator_fee_amount/driver_net_amount`, `operators.association_id`, `drivers.operator_id` (nullable in schema, always set in practice), `uv_slots.status` (gained `boarding`/`departed`), `vehicles.bus_number/service_type_id/capacity` (capacity is type-specific now — even 12–36 for Jeepney, 9–18 for UV Express including the driver (raised from an initial 9–12 in migration 020), NULL-until-seeded for Bus — see Core Flow 13), `fare_matrix.service_type_id`. Migration 020 (Core Flow 12/12a/13/15): added `bus_schedules` (association-owned recurring Bus templates); added `trips.schedule_id` (FK, nullable) and `trips.boarding_time`; added `operators.remittance_mode`/`operator_fee_pct` (per-operator Bus/UV cut, quota-or-percentage); changed `remittance_rates`'s `default` rows to a flat `system_fee_pct=5.00` (no more per-operator `operator_override` — the system's cut isn't operator-negotiable, only the operator's own cut is); widened `vehicles` UV Express capacity check to 9–18; widened `subscription_rates.frequency`/`subscription_payments.frequency` to include `daily`. Migration 021 (Core Flow 16): added `reservations.base_fare_amount` (pre-discount, pre-rounding fare, used to reconstruct full/discounted fare exactly for the driver's discount-override scanner); widened `wallet_transactions.reference_type` to include `discount_surcharge`/`discount_cashback`. Migration 022 (Core Flow 8/8a): added `operator_association_applications.vehicle_type` (Jeepney|UV Express|Bus, backfilled from each row's operator's legacy `operator_type` at migration time) — membership is per-vehicle-type now, not fleet-level; added the 11 `doc_*_url` document columns (see Core Flow 8a); widened `operator_association_applications.status` to include `removed` (operator-leave/association-kick as a terminal state with history, replacing the old silent `operators.association_id = NULL` reset). `operators.operator_type`/`association_id` and `associations.association_type` are deprecated as of this migration — kept, never dropped, no longer written by any current code path. Migration 023 (Core Flow 8): added `operators.registration_document_url` — registration still requires one generic document (valid ID/business permit) for the admin to verify the account against, separate from the vehicle-type-specific checklist on `operator_association_applications`. Migration 024 (Core Flow 13): added `operator_class_details` — operator's own amenities overlay on an association-owned Bus class, never a write to `bus_service_types`. Migration 025 (Core Flow 13, Vehicle Management/Seat Layout Builder): added `vehicles.vehicle_number` (generic identifier, all 3 types); formally brought the pre-existing, previously migration-untracked `seats` table under tracking, widened its `seat_type` enum to add `driver`/`conductor`, added `is_blocked`, and added the `UNIQUE (vehicle_id, seat_label)` constraint it had been missing (see `seats` table note above — this table already held live seat data before the migration, so the migration is written as idempotent `ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS`/dedup-then-`ADD UNIQUE INDEX`, not a plain `CREATE TABLE`); added `bus_layout_templates.name` and dropped its old `UNIQUE (operator_id, service_type_id, capacity)` constraint (superseded by the named Seat Layout Library model, see Do Not Break rule #26); added `bus_layout_template_seats` (a template's actual per-seat data, previously nonexistent — `bus_layout_templates` only stored `capacity`/`layout_style`, not real seat positions). Migration 026 (Core Flow 13): widened `seats.seat_type` and `bus_layout_template_seats.seat_type` to add `blank` — a non-bookable placeholder marking the aisle path itself in a generated layout, excluded from capacity math everywhere and never selectable by a commuter. Migration 027 (Core Flow 8b): added `uv_operating_hours` (association-defined daily start/end-of-operations window per UV Express route, one row per route via `UNIQUE route_id`). Migration 028 (Core Flow 8b rewrite): added `recurrence_type`/`weekdays` to `uv_operating_hours` (daily/specific-days, same shape as `bus_schedules`); added `uv_schedule_activations` (an operator's activate/deactivate toggle per association schedule, `UNIQUE (operator_id, uv_operating_hours_id)`); widened `uv_slots.operator_id` and `uv_slots.capacity` to nullable (a commuter-triggered pooled slot has neither — route-level, uncapped; only legacy operator-created rows still carry real values). Migration 029 (Core Flow 16): added `reservations.boarding_driver_id`/`discount_decision`/`discount_source` — a structured, queryable audit trail (who scanned a reservation, what discount decision was made, and whether the applied discount's provenance is system-verified or a physical-ID grant) for the discount-override scanner, previously reconstructable only by diffing `discount_type` or reading free-text `wallet_transactions` notes. Migration 030 (Org Hierarchy): added `vehicles.association_id` (nullable FK) — an operator can now hold the same vehicle type approved at multiple simultaneous associations, so a vehicle needs its own association link rather than deriving it solely from `operator_id` + `type`; backfilled from each vehicle's operator's approved `operator_association_applications` row for that vehicle's own type. A vehicle's association is permanent once set (never reassigned after creation). Migration 031 (Core Flow 12/15): added `operator_association_wallets` (`operator_id, association_id, balance`, `UNIQUE (operator_id, association_id)`) — splits an operator's earnings per-association now that multi-association membership exists; `operators.wallet_balance` is frozen in place as a non-growing "Legacy" bucket (pre-split money, never credited again, still separately withdrawable). Added `association_id` to `quota_submissions`, `driver_quotas`, `wallet_transactions`, and `withdrawal_requests` (nullable — NULL means the frozen Legacy bucket or a transaction type that can't be attributed to one, e.g. commuter/system rows). Migration 032 (Remittance & Wallet): extended `withdrawal_requests` to support driver-initiated withdrawals alongside operator ones — added `owner_type ENUM('operator','driver')` (existing rows default to `'operator'`), relaxed `operator_id` to nullable, added `driver_id` (nullable FK). A driver's own `wallet_balance` stays a single flat column (never split per-association like an operator's) since a driver only ever has one vehicle/operator/association at a time — see `drivers/driver_withdraw.php`. Migration 033 (Core Flow 9a): added `reservations.resolved_at` (nullable `DATETIME`) — set the moment a reservation leaves the active/reserved state (refund, late cancellation, no-show forfeiture, trip/slot completion or operator-cancellation), giving the commuter-facing Recent Activity feed and Booking History screen a real timestamp to order/display a "Booking Cancelled"/"Trip Completed" event by, which nothing previously captured. Migration 034 (Core Flow 12a's 2026-08-02 rewrite — system-generated Bus trips): relaxed `trips.operator_id`/`vehicle_id`/`driver_id`/`capacity` to nullable (a freshly system-generated trip has none of these until an operator assigns them) and widened `trips.status` to add `'pending_assignment'` as the new default (existing rows are unaffected — every one of them already had all four fields populated); added `trips.conductor_name`/`conductor_phone` (simple nullable text fields — no dedicated conductor table/role exists elsewhere in this schema, kept deliberately minimal). Deliberately did **not** add a `UNIQUE (schedule_id, departure_time)` constraint — this database already had real duplicate rows for that pair from before the rewrite (the old flow never checked per-schedule-per-day uniqueness), and adding a hard constraint would have required deleting pre-existing data, which this app never does silently; duplicate-prevention for newly generated trips is enforced at the application level instead (`config/trip_generation.php`'s own `SELECT`-before-`INSERT` check). Migration 035 (Core Flow 9a point 8): added `rebook_requests` — a commuter-initiated flag on a `no_show` booking asking for a rebook, surfaced only as a badge on the operator's own manifest (this app has no notification system); `UNIQUE (reservation_id, status)` blocks duplicate pending requests per booking. Migration 036 (Core Flow 13a): added `ltfrb_fare_guides` — global, read-only, no-owner reference table for official LTFRB fare guide PDFs, seeded once directly by the migration itself; no API endpoint anywhere writes to it. Migration 037 (Core Flow 15a): widened `paymongo_sources` to track a driver-owned top-up alongside the pre-existing commuter one — added nullable `driver_id` (FK→drivers), relaxed `commuter_id` to nullable; exactly one of the two is set per row. Migration 038 (Core Flow 2a): added `commuter_id_verifications` — the automated OCR/face-match/liveness pipeline's storage table, pilot-scoped to Student only at this point (`category ENUM('student','pwd','elderly')`, only `'student'` ever written yet). Deliberately no UNIQUE constraint on `student_id_number` (an earlier version had one and broke renewal — a legitimate resubmission needs a second row for the same real number, not a rejected insert); different-commuter duplicate prevention enforced at the application level instead. Migration 039: added `commuter_id_verifications.semester` (Student/college only). Migration 040 (Core Flow 2a, category-switch): generalized the table beyond Student — renamed the `category` enum value `'pwd'`→`'disabled'` (matching `commuters.category`'s authoritative naming; only `'student'` rows existed yet, so the rename was safe), widened `verification_status` to add `'pending_review'`, relaxed Student-only fields to nullable, added the generic `id_number` (PWD/Senior, deliberately separate from `student_id_number`) and `birthdate` (Senior only) columns. Migration 041 (Core Flow 2a, Senior Citizen registration): added `commuter_id_verifications.issuing_lgu` and `date_issued` — the two OSCA-card fields the category-switch pipeline hadn't collected yet, both Senior-only, both free-entered (never OCR-cross-checked, unlike name/id_number/birthdate). Migration 042 (Core Flow 2a, PWD registration + renewability): added `commuter_id_verifications.validity_date` (PWD-only, OCR-cross-checked via `checkPwdValidity()`) — unlike Senior Citizen (which reused `issuing_lgu` as-is but never expires), PWD's own registration/renewal endpoints write this column's value into `expires_at` (as `"{validity_date} 23:59:59"`, same-day-inclusive), making PWD the second renewable category alongside Student. Migration 043 (Registration Redesign): added structured personal-name/DOB columns (`first_name`/`middle_name`/`last_name`/`suffix`/`date_of_birth`) to `commuters` and `operators` (both nullable — `name` itself is untouched and still populated, concatenated from these at registration, so every existing reader of `.name` keeps working unchanged); added `operators.business_name`/`business_address` (later partially reverted — see migration 044) and a full Authorized Representative + org-contact + document set to `associations` (`rep_first_name`/`rep_middle_name`/`rep_last_name`/`rep_suffix`/`rep_position`, `acronym`, `official_email`/`official_phone`, `supporting_document_url`, later replaced — see migration 044); added `consent_acceptances` (polymorphic `account_type`/`account_id`, `terms_version`/`privacy_version`/`accepted_at` — one row per successful registration, versioned so a future policy change can require re-acceptance without losing the history of what an account originally agreed to). Migration 044 (Registration Redesign refinement, same-day follow-up): dropped `operators.business_address` from the registration form only (an operator isn't tied to one association's address, since it can apply to multiple associations including multiple of the same vehicle type) — the column itself stays in place, unpopulated for new rows, deprecate-in-place; added `operators.entity_type` (`individual`|`business`, explicitly distinct from the deprecated vehicle-type `operator_type` column) and `operators.business_phone`; added `associations.rep_government_id_url` (the Authorized Representative's own required government ID, distinct from `document_url` which proves the organization itself) — `supporting_document_url` (migration 043) is likewise no longer collected by the form but stays in place, unpopulated, same convention. Migration 045 (Core Flow 2b, Commuter Settings): added `commuters.deactivated_at` — reversible self-service account deactivation, deliberately a separate column from `status`/`rejected_at` (which already mean admin-side rejection in `login_mobile.php`'s commuter branch); cleared automatically the next time the commuter logs back in with the correct password, no admin step either direction. Superseded in the Settings UI the same day by migration 046's Delete Account — left in place, dormant, per this codebase's deprecate-in-place convention. Migration 046 (Core Flow 2b, same-day Settings redesign): added `commuters.deleted_at` — permanent soft-delete, a separate column from both `status`/`rejected_at` and `deactivated_at`. `commuter/delete_account.php` sets it alongside anonymizing every PII column on the row; nothing else (wallets, reservations, wallet_transactions, commuter_id_verifications, consent_acceptances) is touched. Migration 047 (Core Flow 9.4's 2026-08-10 rebooking rewrite): widened `rebook_requests.status` from `(pending|fulfilled|dismissed)` to `(pending|approved|rejected|fulfilled|expired)` (`'dismissed'` rows remapped to `'rejected'` first, since MySQL silently blanks a stored value no longer in a new ENUM's list); added `rebook_requests.reviewed_by_operator_id`/`reviewed_at`/`rejection_reason` (an audit trail the old model never needed, since the operator used to act atomically rather than in a separate review step); added `reservations.rebooking_fee_amount` (persists the 10% fee actually charged, for audit — mirrors `system_fee_amount`/`operator_fee_amount`/`driver_net_amount`'s existing pattern rather than recomputing from `REBOOKING_SURCHARGE_PCT` later, since that rate could change over time). Migration 049 (Core Flow 8f, Profile Picture / Avatar Upload): added nullable `avatar_url` to `commuters`, `drivers`, `operators`, and `associations` — a self-uploaded photo shown on each role's own Profile screen, purely cosmetic, never read by any verification/fare/matching logic. Stored under a new, dedicated `uploads/avatars/` folder (kept separate from each role's existing verification-document upload folder). Migration 050 (Core Flow 9's 2026-08-11 tiered-refund rewrite): added `reservations.refund_amount`/`refund_pct` (both nullable) — the actual peso amount and percentage credited back to the commuter on a `'refunded'` cancellation, needed because `cancellation_type='refunded'` no longer implies a full refund (it now covers both the 100% and 50% tiers, see `config/refund.php`'s `computeRefundOutcome()`); `NULL` for a `'late_cancelled'`/`'no_show'` (0%) outcome. Also stamped as `100.00`/full-fare on the pre-existing operator-cancellation paths (`bus_trips/update_trip_status.php`, `uv_slots/update_slot_status.php`) for display consistency, though those paths' own always-100% behavior is unchanged by this migration. Migration 051 (Core Flow 8g, Jeepney Operating Hours): added `jeepney_operating_hours` — an association-published daily operating window per Jeepney route, purely informational (unlike `uv_operating_hours`, nothing in booking/dispatch reads it), shown to commuters via a map marker tap and to drivers on their Dispatch tab.

---

# Mobile App — Expo Router Structure

**Session bootstrap** (`app/_layout.tsx`):
- On mount, reads `AsyncStorage.getItem('user')` + `AsyncStorage.getItem('role')`
- Redirects: `commuter` → `/(main)/home`, `operator` → `/(operator)/dashboard`, `association` → `/(association)/dashboard`, `driver` → `/(driver)/dashboard`

## Screen Routes

### `/(auth)/`
- `onboard.tsx` — Role selection landing
- `login.tsx` — Login form → `login_mobile.php`
- `register.tsx` — Role picker (commuter/driver/operator/association); operator now routes straight to `operator-register.tsx` (no intermediate type step)
- `commuter-register.tsx`, `operator-register.tsx`, `association-register.tsx` — Role-specific forms. No "Driver" option on the role picker — driver accounts are created by an operator (Drivers screen → Add Driver), not self-registered. `operator-register.tsx` is now **generic** — name/email/phone/password only, no vehicle type, no association picker, no document upload (all moved to the post-registration apply flow, see Core Flow 8.2). `operator-type.tsx` (the old Jeepney/UV/Bus selection step) was **removed** — vehicle type is chosen per association application instead, not once at registration. Selecting the Student, PWD, or Senior discount type on `commuter-register.tsx` collects only email/phone/password here (stashed via `src/state/registrationDraft.ts`, a plain in-memory module, not a Context) and routes straight into that category's own dedicated wizard instead of showing the plain "Valid ID" upload field — the legacy gallery-upload path is left in place, deprecated-in-place, but no discount type reaches it from the UI anymore.
- `student-verification.tsx` — Multi-step Student ID wizard (camera-only ID front/back capture, auto OCR pre-check, conditional Enrollment Form step, 3-pose facial liveness capture, review/submit). Dual-purpose via `?mode=renew` — either registers a brand-new account from the draft or renews an already-logged-in commuter's lapsed verification. See Core Flow 2a.
- `pwd-verification.tsx` — Multi-step PWD ID wizard, structurally a merge of `senior-verification.tsx`'s wizard shape and `student-verification.tsx`'s `?mode=renew` dual-purpose pattern — Last/First/Middle Name, PWD ID Number, Issuing LGU, Validity Date → camera-only ID front/back → 3-pose facial liveness → review/submit, calling `auth/commuter_register_pwd.php` (fresh registration, draft-based) or `auth/commuter_renew_pwd.php` (`?mode=renew`, commuter_id read from AsyncStorage). Unlike Student's binary renewal, both modes here branch on a 4-outcome `data.outcome` (`verified`/`pending_review`) — a renewal landing `pending_review` shows an inline "Submitted for Review" state (no next screen to carry the message to, unlike registration's `register-success`). See Core Flow 2a.
- `senior-verification.tsx` — Multi-step Senior Citizen ID wizard for **fresh registration** (structurally `(main)/category-switch.tsx`'s `elderly` branch ported to a no-existing-account context) — Last/First/Middle Name, Senior Citizen ID Number, Issuing LGU, Date Issued, Birthdate → camera-only ID front/back → 3-pose facial liveness → review/submit, calling `auth/commuter_register_senior.php`. See Core Flow 2a.
- `register-success.tsx` — Confirmation. Shows "Pending Verification" (with an inline reason note) instead of "Verified" whenever the just-completed Student/Senior registration landed as `pending_review` rather than a clean auto-pass (`?note=` param) — every other category's registration is always pending by default and never sets this param.

### `/(main)/` — Commuter tabs (custom tab bar: home|map|book|wallet|profile). Wrapped in `UserProvider` (outermost) then `BookingsProvider` (`(main)/_layout.tsx`, around `<Tabs>`) — see Core Flow 2 point 7 for the shared live-verification-status context, Core Flow 9a for the shared booking-sync context.
- `home.tsx` — Wallet balance, quick actions, route browser, journey planner. "Recent Activity" is wired to `BookingsContext`'s derived activity feed (previously a hardcoded, permanently-empty placeholder — see Core Flow 9a), refetched on focus plus an 18s poll while this tab stays open; empty state "No recent activity." The Verified/Pending/Rejected badge and discount-status card read `UserContext` (Core Flow 2 point 7) instead of a one-time AsyncStorage snapshot, so an admin verifying this commuter mid-session updates them without a re-login. Pull-to-refresh (`RefreshControl` on the root `ScrollView`) re-fetches wallet balance, the route list (or the active journey search, if one's in progress), Recent Activity, and this profile status together.
- `map.tsx` — Live map with nearby drivers (Jeepney only within 5km). Tapping a driver marker (added 2026-08-12, Core Flow 8g — previously non-interactive) opens a Driver Detail Sheet showing the driver's route and, if the association published one, that route's operating hours — purely informational, no booking/dispatch effect. Route Suggestions cards for an all-Jeepney option show "Operating Hours: …" (plus "Currently not operating" when outside that window) instead of the old driver-availability "No drivers" badge (also 2026-08-12) — Bus/UV Express options keep the original badge.
- `book.tsx` — Pre-booking interface; Bus trip cards/detail show the class name and, if the operator has added one, an amenities/details blurb (see Core Flow 13); Bus seat picker renders a positioned grid (by each seat's actual row/col) with the 5-color scheme (Green=Available, Red=Booked, Blue=Selected, Yellow=Reserved/Saved i.e. operator-blocked, Gray=Driver/Conductor), sourced from `get_available_seats.php`'s derived `state` field. UV Express "Book Ahead" (see Core Flow 8b) still lists already-created upcoming slots via `get_available_slots.php` (so commuters converge on shared times), but now also offers a "Pick Your Own Time" date+time stepper (same visual pattern as the Bus date picker) backed by `routes/get_uv_schedule.php` (operating hours/recurrence/`is_bookable`) — confirming calls `uv_slots/resolve_slot.php` to find-or-create the pooled slot before proceeding to the passenger form, same as picking an existing one. The Bus seat-picking `useEffect` is keyed on the selected trip only, never on wizard `step` — it used to also fire on every step change and unconditionally clear the just-picked seat before the commuter reached "Confirm Booking," silently no-opping the submit with zero feedback (a real reported bug, fixed). "My Upcoming Bookings" (each mode's first/route-picking stage) now reads from `BookingsContext` instead of its own local fetch (see Core Flow 9a) — refetches on tab focus **plus an 18s poll while the tab stays open** (`UPCOMING_POLL_MS`, Core Flow 9a point 10), shows "You don't have any upcoming trips." when empty (previously the whole section just didn't render), shows each booking's seat label(s) inline when set (Core Flow 9a point 11), and a new booking appears instantly via `addOptimistic()` right after `create_reservation.php` succeeds, without needing "Book Another Trip". Its status badge (and Booking History's and Booking Details') now render the shared `getDisplayStatus()` vocabulary — Upcoming/Boarding/Completed/Cancelled/Refunded/Rebooked/Expired, Core Flow 9a point 9 — not the raw `boarding_status`. Tapping any card opens `BookingDetailsModal` (shared with `booking-history.tsx`, see Core Flow 9a point 8) — Cancel/Request Rebook now live inside that shared component instead of two inline modals local to this file.
- `booking-history.tsx` — New screen (not in the tab bar, pushed from `profile.tsx`'s "My Bookings" menu item). Lists every booking (`scope=all`) via `BookingsContext`, newest-created first; empty state "No booking history yet." See Core Flow 9a. Cards are now tappable, opening the same shared `BookingDetailsModal` as "My Upcoming Bookings" (Core Flow 9a point 8) — previously these cards were static, non-interactive. Refetches on focus plus an 18s poll while the screen stays open (Core Flow 9a point 10), same cadence as `home.tsx`'s Recent Activity.
- `wallet.tsx` — Balance + transaction history + top-up via GCash (Demo)/Maya (Demo), both through the same PayMongo `create_source.php`/`verify_payment.php` flow — see Core Flow 15a
- `profile.tsx` — Settings, category, document resubmission. "My Bookings" menu item now pushes to `booking-history.tsx` (previously a dead `onPress={() => {}}` stub, see Core Flow 9a); "Wallet History" is still a dead stub, unrelated to this change. Verified badge/Status row read `UserContext` and refetch on tab focus (Core Flow 2 point 7) — no longer a one-time AsyncStorage read at mount. New "LTFRB Fare Guide" menu item pushes to `ltfrb-fare-guide.tsx` (Core Flow 13a). "Verification Status" pushes to `verification-status.tsx` and "Settings" pushes to `settings.tsx` (both previously dead stubs — see Core Flow 2b). "Change Discount Type" button (only shown once `user.can_switch_category` is true — i.e. the commuter's current category's verification has expired) pushes to `category-switch.tsx`. The expired-discount card's "Renew Verification" button routes by `raw_category` — `pwd-verification.tsx` for `disabled`, `student-verification.tsx` otherwise (Senior never expires, so it never reaches this button) — both with `?mode=renew`. A green "Eligible for a discount?" card (added 2026-09-20, shown only when `user.can_apply_category` is true — mutually exclusive with the expired-discount card above) offers Student/PWD/Senior buttons straight into the same three wizards, mirroring Settings' pre-existing "Discount Eligibility" section (Core Flow 2b point 6) so a first-time Standard commuter can find it without a detour through Settings.
- `category-switch.tsx` — Not a tab; reached only from `profile.tsx`'s "Change Discount Type" action. Lets an already-logged-in commuter with an expired verification switch to `default` (instant) or re-verify into `disabled`/`elderly` via the same OCR/face-match/liveness pipeline Student registration uses (see Core Flow 2a) — camera-only ID front/back + 3-pose facial liveness + review/submit, calling `auth/commuter_switch_category.php`. For `elderly`, also collects Issuing LGU/Date Issued/Birthdate; for `disabled` (PWD), also collects Issuing LGU/Validity Date.
- `ltfrb-fare-guide.tsx` — New screen (not in the tab bar). Lists every official LTFRB fare guide PDF grouped by vehicle type, opened via `expo-web-browser`'s in-app browser sheet. Read-only reference only — see Core Flow 13a.
- `verification-status.tsx` — Not a tab; reached only from `profile.tsx`'s "Verification Status" item. Read-only status summary, pure presentation over `UserContext` (Core Flow 2b) — no new fetch of its own.
- `select-rebook-trip.tsx` — Not a tab; reached only from `BookingDetailsModal`'s "Select New Trip" button, itself shown only once an operator has approved a rebook request (Core Flow 9.4). Scoped to the original booking's own operator (any route, matching vehicle category) via the `operator_id` filter on `get_upcoming_trips.php`/`get_available_slots.php`; a self-contained Bus seat-grid (deliberately duplicated from `book.tsx`'s inline picker rather than extracted into a shared component, to avoid refactor risk to that already-complex screen) → confirm (shows the original fare + 10% fee) → `complete_rebook.php`. No passenger-info step — already on file from the original booking.
- `settings.tsx` — Not a tab; reached only from `profile.tsx`'s "Settings" item. Five sections: Account Information (edit Name/Email/Phone together), Notifications (four local display-preference toggles — Recent Activity filtering by kind, plus a genuinely new verification-status-change banner), Privacy & Security (Change Password + static security/session info), Terms & Policies (read-only Terms/Privacy/Cancellation viewers, reusing registration/booking content), Delete Account (permanent soft-delete — see Core Flow 2b).
- **Profile Picture** (`profile.tsx`, added 2026-08-11) — the avatar circle is now a tappable `AvatarPicker` (`src/components/common/AvatarPicker.tsx`, shared across all four roles) instead of an initials-only circle; tap opens Take Photo/Choose from Library/Remove Photo, uploads via `commuter/update_avatar.php`, and reconciles through the existing `UserContext.refreshUser()` (Core Flow 8f).

### `/(driver)/`
- `dashboard.tsx` — Queue UI (join/leave/start/end; joining is blocked for a Jeepney driver with an overdue subscription, see Core Flow 15), trip status, daily summary, passenger list; the Dispatch tab shows a "Route Operating Hours" card directly below "Assigned Route" for Jeepney drivers only (added 2026-08-12, Core Flow 8g) — purely informational, reads whatever the association published via `routes/get_jeepney_operating_hours.php`; Bus panel shows a time-aware boarding/departure hint line (never hard-disables the buttons, see Core Flow 11); Wallet tab has a "Platform Subscription" card for Jeepney drivers only (see Core Flow 15) plus a uniform Withdraw button/modal and a Withdrawal Requests status list (Pending/Completed/Rejected) for **every** driver type — no vehicle-type gating, backed by `drivers/driver_withdraw.php` (see Remittance & Wallet API table). "Top Up" now runs the same real PayMongo GCash (Demo)/Maya (Demo) checkout flow as the commuter wallet, replacing the old instant no-gateway credit (Core Flow 15a). No seat-type-options card on Settings anymore (removed — decorative, not backed by anything real). The "Upcoming Bus Trip" panel shows a "Mark Arrived" button (calls `complete`, same transition the operator side uses) once a trip is `departed` — this surfaced a real bug: `bus_trips/get_driver_trips.php` only queried `status IN ('scheduled','boarding')`, so a departed trip vanished from the driver's own view the moment they tapped Depart, making the (already-written) "Departed" badge code unreachable and leaving no way to ever mark it arrived; fixed by including `'departed'` in that query too. **UV Express drivers have no Go Online/Go Offline toggle** (removed — see Core Flow 8b point 8): the old "UV Express Booking" live-session card is gone entirely; the "UV Express Passengers" card (backed by `uv_slots/get_slot_passengers.php`, unchanged) is now the sole UV Express interface — a driver joins the shared dispatch queue same as before, and once dispatched, boards whichever commuters already booked for that route via "Scan to Board." The driver has no availability-control action of their own. **The internal "Settings" tab is also this app's Driver Profile** (there's no separate Profile tab or route-group `_layout.tsx` for `(driver)/` at all) — now shows an avatar/initials circle, a colored Active/Inactive status pill, Driver ID, and (added to the existing Operator Info card) Association and Assigned Vehicle rows, live-refreshed via `drivers/get_profile.php` on tab focus; plus two new rows, "Driver Verification →" and "Account Settings →" (Core Flow 8e).
- `scan-ticket.tsx` — QR scanner for Bus/UV Express reservation boarding; persistent "Discount Mode" toggle + an accept/decline prompt whenever a scanned ticket was already booked with a discount, letting the driver correct it based on whether a physical ID was actually shown (surcharge on decline, cashback on grant — see Core Flow 16). Result modal also shows a purple "REBOOKED PASSENGER" info block (Original Fare / Rebooking Fee Paid / Additional Fare Due: ₱0) whenever the scanned ticket is a continuation of an earlier rebooked-away booking (`is_rebooked`, see Core Flow 9.4) — informational only, no discount accept/decline prompt ever shows for these (already gated off server-side, `payment_status` is already `released`)
- `driver-map.tsx` — Map with assigned route waypoints. **No QR code UI of any kind** (removed 2026-08-02, all vehicle types incl. Jeepney — see Core Flow 4) — focused solely on navigation/location/trip monitoring. QR now exists only in `(driver)/scan-ticket.tsx` (Driver QR Scanner, Bus/UV Express boarding) and the commuter's Upcoming Bookings ticket view.
- `driver-verification.tsx` — Not a tab; reached only from the Settings tab's "Driver Verification" row. Read-only, shows `status`-derived Active/Inactive state, with copy explicitly noting it's separate from association membership and vehicle assignment (Core Flow 8e).
- `settings.tsx` — Not a tab; reached only from the Settings tab's "Account Settings" row. Four sections: Account Information (Name/Email/Phone, Driver ID read-only), Notifications (6 inert local preference toggles), Privacy & Security (Change Password — a new capability, this role had none before), Terms & Policies (new `DRIVER_TERMS`/Privacy Policy viewers). No account-closure action anywhere (Core Flow 8e).
- `earnings-history.tsx` — Not a tab; reached only from the Settings tab's Earnings card (Bus/UV Express only) → "View Earnings History". Read-only 30-day per-day earnings breakdown + grand total, backed by `drivers/earnings_history.php` (added 2026-08-10, see Core Flow 8e's addendum).

### `/(operator)/` — Operator tabs (dashboard|drivers|vehicles|trips|quota|profile)
- `dashboard.tsx` — Operator stats; a "Vehicle Types" card lists every vehicle type ever applied for with a per-type status chip (✓/…/!), replacing the old single fixed `operator_type` display — "operational" (unlocks Manage Drivers) is now true if *any* vehicle type is approved, not tied to one type. **De-duplicated by `vehicle_type`** (`dedupeByVehicleType()`, approved beats pending beats other) before rendering — an operator can hold several `operator_association_applications` rows for the *same* type (one per association applied to), so the raw list could otherwise render "Bus" twice with an unstable/duplicate React key; the card shows one chip per distinct type, reflecting the operator's best standing at any association for it
- `apply-association.tsx` — **New.** Pick a vehicle type (Jeepney/UV Express/Bus) → pick any active association (unfiltered — associations aren't typed anymore) → upload that type's own document checklist (4/4/5 fields, see Core Flow 8a) → submit. Reachable from Profile → "My Associations" → "Apply". An operator can run this repeatedly for different vehicle types (even to different associations).
- `drivers.tsx` — Driver list management ("Add Driver" — operator sets the login credentials directly and hands them to the driver); Edit button per driver (name/email/phone/license/**password**/**assigned vehicle**, see Core Flow 14); gated on having *any* approved vehicle-type membership (a driver record has no type of its own until a vehicle is assigned). Each driver card also shows a route chip (with inline unassign) once assigned, or an "Assign Route" button once the driver has a vehicle (see Core Flow 8.11) — the picker is a plain `.map()`/`View` list, not `FlatList`, matching the rest of this file's small bounded pickers.
- `vehicles.tsx` — **Vehicle Management module**, all 3 types. The "Add Vehicle" type picker is an open 3-way choice (Jeepney/UV Express/Bus), each option locked unless that specific type has an approved association membership (checked live, not derived from one fixed `operator_type`); every type now also requires a `vehicle_number` (fleet-internal identifier, distinct from plate number); capacity picker is otherwise still type-specific (Jeepney 12–36 even, UV Express 9–18 incl. driver). Each vehicle card shows plate/number/type/capacity/status/assigned route(s) (read-through of the driver's route assignment) and an Edit action (`vehicle_number`, plus Bus's `bus_number`/class, via `update_details`) — **the class picker is greyed out with a hint once the vehicle has a seat layout**, since changing class is blocked server-side until the layout is cleared (see Core Flow 13). Bus vehicles pick a class, then registration **chains straight into the Seat Layout Builder** for the new vehicle (Bus-only) instead of leaving it to find later — a positioned grid where the operator can add/move/remove/rename individual seats (incl. designated Driver/Conductor seats and a per-seat "Reserved/Saved" block toggle). The builder's empty state offers **"Generate Standard Layout"** / **"Generate Sleeper Layout"** (the new `generateLayout()` algorithm — 2×2+aisle or odd-count 5-seat bench for standard, stacked Lower/Upper berths for sleeper, driver/conductor always included, a real `blank` seat marks the aisle path itself between the left/right pairs — see Core Flow 13 — replaces the old flat capacity+style "Quick Generate" modal) alongside the **Seat Layout Library** picker/saver (`list_seat_templates.php`/`save_seat_template.php`/`apply_seat_template.php`/`delete_seat_template.php`) — applying a template makes an independent copy, editable without affecting the template or other vehicles that applied it (see Core Flow 13, supersedes Do Not Break rule #26's old silent-reuse model) — and a **"Clear Layout"** action (the only path back to "no layout", needed to unblock a class change). Generating (or hand-editing) only changes local state — a persistent amber banner appears whenever there are unsaved changes, since a real point of confusion was an operator generating a layout, seeing it in the review grid, and never tapping "Save Layout," leaving the vehicle's capacity unset despite looking configured. A seat with an active booking can't be deleted (only repositioned/retyped) in the builder, when applying a template, or when clearing a layout.
- `trips.tsx` — Shows a vehicle-type chip switcher when the operator holds more than one approved type (derived live from `association_status.php`, not a fixed field) — each vehicle type resolves to exactly one association, so this single chip switcher doubles as "which association am I managing right now"; there is no second tab layer on top of it. Selecting **UV Express** shows a "Your UV Schedules" section (one card per association-published schedule: route, operating hours, recurrence, Activate/Deactivate toggle via `operators/uv_schedule_activation.php`, see Core Flow 8b — UV Express has no trip/slot-creation form at all anymore) directly above the existing "Route Queue" panel per active route (current dispatched driver + waiting list, "Advance to Next Vehicle" button — layers on the existing `dispatch_queue`, see the UV Express dispatch queue Critical Insight) and the list of upcoming pooled bookings (`fetchOperatorSlots`, now including routes the operator is merely *active* on, not just ones it strictly owns) — all in one scrollable view. Selecting **Bus** is unaffected by any of this — no read-only schedule display was added there. A **"Your Classes"** section (every class the operator's association has defined, from `get_association_service_types.php`) renders directly on the main Bus tab (loaded alongside the trip list itself, not tied to any modal — moved here from the old "Add Trip" modal in the 2026-08-02 rewrite, see Core Flow 12a) regardless of schedule selection — each with its own "Add/Edit Details" action (operator's own amenities overlay, see Core Flow 13); deliberately not nested inside schedule selection, since a class can exist (and need a details blurb) before any schedule for it has been published — an earlier version nested it under the picked schedule and silently made classes with no schedule unreachable for editing. **Bus trip creation is now Assign, not Add** (Core Flow 12a's 2026-08-02 rewrite) — the header's old "+ Add" button is gone; each system-generated `pending_assignment` trip card shows its own "Assign Trip" button, opening a modal scoped to that one trip (route/date/class shown read-only) with just a vehicle picker (filtered to the trip's own class+association, matching driver auto-derived from the vehicle's own fleet-level assignment same as before) and optional conductor name/phone fields — no schedule picker, no date field, no next-occurrence preview, since the trip and its date already exist. Every non-"all", non-"pending" status tab's trip list is grouped by date (soonest first) via `groupTripsByDate()` (each such list only ever has one status in it, so date is the only useful sort axis). **The "All" tab is instead a status-grouped operational dashboard** (`groupTripsByStatus()`, see Core Flow 12a point 8) — Ready/Open → Boarding → Departed → Completed → Cancelled sections, each internally date/time-sorted, plus a tappable Pending Assignment count summary banner at the top (never inline pending cards — see below). **The "Pending Assignment" status chip is date-scoped, not part of either grouped view** (rewritten 2026-08-02 — see Core Flow 12a point 7): selecting it swaps the trip-list area into a date-nav view — prev/next arrows + the selected date's full label on top (fine one-day adjustment), and a **horizontal scrollable strip of all `PENDING_DATE_OPTIONS`** below it (every date in the window, each a directly-tappable chip labeled TODAY/TMRW/weekday+day-number) for jumping straight to any date without stepping through the arrow one day at a time. Backed by `bus_trips/get_pending_trips.php`, fetched fresh per date instead of filtered client-side from an already-loaded list; backward navigation stops at today, forward at `PENDING_WINDOW_DAYS` (60, mirrors the server's own generation window) on both the arrows and the strip. **Every trip card (pending, date-grouped, and status-grouped alike) renders through one shared `TripCard` component** (module-level, see Core Flow 12a point 8 for the full UI/UX pass this came from) — action buttons are strict per lifecycle stage now (Ready/Open no longer shows Depart, Boarding no longer shows Cancel), and the card-overlap/button-overflow bug from the first version of this screen is fixed there (`tripCard` gained `marginBottom`, `tripActions` gained `flexWrap`). Trip/slot manifest shows Approve/Reject buttons (Reject requires a typed reason) plus an amber "Commuter requested a rebook" badge whenever `rebook_requested` is true (Core Flow 9a point 8) — regardless of the booking's `boarding_status`, since eligibility is no longer `no_show`-only (Core Flow 9.4). Approving only grants permission; the commuter picks their own new trip/seat and pays the fee separately from their own Booking Details screen (`select-rebook-trip.tsx`) — the operator no longer picks the new trip here at all (a real behavior change from the old model, which had its own inline trip/seat-picker modal in this file, now removed entirely). The vehicle picker inside the Assign Trip modal is a plain `.map()`/`View` list inside a `ScrollView`, not `FlatList` — matches the rest of this file's small bounded pickers, per the same "VirtualizedLists nested inside ScrollViews" fix already applied elsewhere. The manifest list (potentially dozens of passengers) is intentionally left as a standalone `FlatList` — it's not nested in a ScrollView and does benefit from windowing. A Ready/Open (`scheduled`) trip card's action row also gained a **"Reassign"** button (next to Start Boarding/Cancel) that reopens the same Assign Trip modal, pre-filled with the trip's current vehicle/conductor, calling `bus_trips/reassign_trip.php` instead of `assign_trip.php` — lets an operator swap a broken-down vehicle or a driver who called in sick without the trip reverting to Pending Assignment (see Core Flow 12a point 3a).
- `quota.tsx` — header titled "Driver Quotas"; three tabs underneath, universal across every operator type: **Targets** (the peso remittance target, Vehicle Type → Route → Driver, always-visible locked chips for unapproved types — rewritten 2026-09-20, see Core Flow 12b), **Submissions** (remittance review, Confirm/Reject — the old combined "Remittance" tab's own Targets sub-tab was removed as redundant with this standalone tab), and **Wallet** (balance/withdraw/transaction history). The separate booking-capacity-cap UI ("Limits" tab, `driver_route_quotas`) that briefly lived on this screen alongside the rewrite was removed the same day — its backend stays fully live and enforced, just unreachable from any current screen (Core Flow 12b point 6). Plus a "Your Cut Per Booking" card shown whenever the operator has any Bus/UV Express driver context (derived from their drivers' own vehicle types, not Jeepney-gated by a fixed account field) toggling `remittance_mode` quota vs. percentage and setting `operator_fee_pct` (see Core Flow 12)
- `fare.tsx` — Shows a vehicle-type chip switcher when routes of more than one kind exist. Jeepney/UV Express: unchanged editable flat per-route fare. Bus: **read-only** now — shows the association-set per-class fares for the operator's *Bus-approved* association's routes; no LTFRB guide card anymore (any vehicle kind)
- **Profile Picture (added 2026-08-11)** — the avatar circle on `profile.tsx` is now a tappable `AvatarPicker` (shared component, Core Flow 8f) instead of initials-only; uploads via `operators/update_avatar.php`.
- `profile.tsx` — Operator settings; "My Associations" is now a **list** — one card per vehicle-type application with its own status badge and "Leave" action (instead of a single "My Association" card), plus an "Apply" entry point into `apply-association.tsx`. Account verification (admin reviews the operator's registration document, `operators.registration_document_url` — see Core Flow 8) is shown separately from association membership, since the two are fully independent steps — the verification card is now tappable, pushing to `operator-verification.tsx` (Core Flow 8d). The quick-links menu's "Manage Routes" item was a dead link (pointed at `/(operator)/routes`, a screen that no longer exists — routes have been association-only since Core Flow 8.11) — replaced with "Manage Vehicles" → `/(operator)/vehicles`; a "Settings" item was added alongside it (Core Flow 8d). Now fetches `operators/get_profile.php` on focus (paint-from-AsyncStorage-then-reconcile) instead of only ever reading the stale login-time snapshot. "My Associations"/Apply/Leave/Switch logic itself: untouched.
- `operator-verification.tsx` — Not a tab; reached only from `profile.tsx`'s verification badge. Read-only, shows `is_verified`/`status`/`rejected_at`-derived state, with copy explicitly noting it's separate from per-association approval (Core Flow 8d).
- `settings.tsx` — Not a tab; reached only from `profile.tsx`'s "Settings" item. Four sections: Account Information (Name/Email/Phone, Operator ID read-only), Notifications (6 inert local preference toggles), Privacy & Security (Change Password — a new capability, this role had none before), Terms & Policies (`OPERATOR_TERMS`/Privacy Policy viewers). No account-closure action anywhere (Core Flow 8d).

### `/(association)/` — Association tabs (dashboard|members|route-builder|fare|schedules|profile)
- `dashboard.tsx` — Reviews incoming **operator** applications (`operator_association_applications`) — pending/approved/rejected tabs, approve/reject with reason (blocked server-side until the admin has verified the operator, see Core Flow 8); each application card and its review modal show `vehicle_type` (not the deprecated `operator_type`) and dynamically list whichever of the 11 possible LTO/LTFRB documents are present for that vehicle type (see Core Flow 8a), each opened in the shared **`DocumentViewerModal`** (`src/components/DocumentViewerModal.tsx`, new 2026-08-05) instead of `Linking.openURL` — a full-screen in-app viewer with real pinch-zoom/pan/double-tap-to-zoom (built on `react-native-gesture-handler` + `react-native-reanimated`, both already installed for `expo-router` and already wrapped by `GestureHandlerRootView` in `app/_layout.tsx` — no new native dependency), explicit prev/next buttons + dot indicator to move between every document the application actually has a URL for (registration document first, then the present vehicle-type checklist fields, in `DOC_LABELS` order — a field genuinely inapplicable to this vehicle type is just absent from the response, not shown as a placeholder), and a "Couldn't load this document" placeholder (not a blank screen) when a stored URL's file fails to load — a real, pre-existing condition confirmed live against 4 orphaned document rows from application id 7 (operator 15, files no longer on disk) out of 26 total document URLs in the current database. Chose explicit buttons over a swipeable pager deliberately — nesting a horizontal-swipe-between-documents gesture with pinch/pan-while-zoomed inside it risks gesture conflicts that aren't practical to get right without on-device testing, which this environment can't do.
- `members.tsx` — Member **operators** (one row per operator, showing every vehicle type approved *at this association* as a badge list), each with their drivers nested underneath (transitive, filtered to those vehicle types); "Remove" now targets one specific vehicle-type membership — a picker appears if the operator has more than one approved type at this association
- `route-builder.tsx` — Create routes (the only path to a Bus route now — see Core Flow 13); the new-terminal vehicle-type default no longer derives from `association_type` (deprecated) — just defaults to Jeepney, since the picker lets the association choose any of the three regardless
- `fare.tsx` — **Always shows all three vehicle-type tabs** (Jeepney/UV Express/Bus, chip-switchable), regardless of whether the association has routes of each kind yet — a kind with none just shows its own "No {kind} routes yet" empty state, since associations aren't typed anymore (`association_type` deprecated). The "Classes" button (Bus catalog) is always reachable in the header regardless of which kind's routes are currently shown. Jeepney/UV Express: unchanged editable flat per-route fare. Bus: owns the class catalog (header "Classes" button → create/delete classes) and a per-route "Manage Class Fares" modal (assign any subset of its classes to a route, each with its own fare, removable independently). A new **"LTFRB Fare Reference" section** (Core Flow 13a, added 2026-08-06 — not a revival of the old removed guide card, see that section's note) sits above the route list, real official LTFRB PDFs viewable in-app per the active vehicle-kind tab, purely informational and never feeding into fare computation
- `schedules.tsx` — Tab switcher between **Bus Schedules**, **UV Operating Hours**, and **Jeepney Hours**. Bus tab: CRUD for recurring Bus schedule templates (route + class + boarding time + departure time + recurrence), backed by `associations/bus_schedules.php` (see Core Flow 12a) — Edit button reopens the create form pre-filled (PUT as a full field update), plus a status toggle and Delete; saving/reactivating auto-generates that schedule's trip window server-side, nothing further to do here. Recurrence is now four preset buttons — **Every Day / Weekdays / Weekends / Custom** — over the same underlying `recurrence_type`/`weekdays` fields (`detectRecurrencePreset()`/`applyRecurrencePreset()`; "Custom" reveals the pre-existing weekday chip picker for manual selection), UI-only, no backend change. UV tab: sets/edits/toggles/removes the operating-hours window **and recurrence** (daily/specific-days, same underlying fields, still the original two-button picker — presets weren't extended here since UV scheduling isn't part of this rewrite) per UV Express route, backed by `associations/uv_operating_hours.php` (see Core Flow 8b) — routes without a schedule yet show a "Set Hours" prompt. Jeepney Hours tab (added 2026-08-12, Core Flow 8g): same set/edit/toggle/remove-per-route pattern as UV, backed by `associations/jeepney_operating_hours.php`, but flat daily hours only — no recurrence fields, since this is purely a display window for commuters/drivers with no booking flow to enforce it against. None of the three tabs are gated behind `association_type` (deprecated) — all are always reachable, showing their own empty state if the association has no routes/classes of that kind yet.
- **Profile Picture (added 2026-08-11)** — the avatar circle is now a tappable `AvatarPicker` (shared component, Core Flow 8f), fallback icon unchanged (business glyph) when no photo is set; uploads via `associations/update_avatar.php`.
- `profile.tsx` — Avatar, association name, a **status pill now reading the real `status`** (Active/Pending/Inactive, previously hardcoded to "Active" regardless of actual state — a real bug fixed alongside this pass), an Association Details card (Name inline-editable exactly as before — pencil → `TextInput` → save, unauthenticated single-field `update_association.php` call, unchanged — Email/Phone/Address stay read-only display here), and two new Profile Actions rows, "Association Verification →" and "Settings →" (see Core Flow "Association Settings"). Now fetches `associations/get_profile.php` on focus (paints from the AsyncStorage snapshot first, same pattern as commuter's `UserContext`) instead of only ever reading the stale login-time snapshot — no Association-equivalent of `UserContext` exists, this is a lightweight per-screen fetch, not a new context provider.
- `association-verification.tsx` — Not a tab; reached only from `profile.tsx`'s "Association Verification" action. Read-only, shows the association's own `status` as Active/Pending/Inactive with explanatory copy — this app has no separate "representative account verification" concept for associations (no `is_verified` column, unlike commuters/operators), so this is the only verification view for this role, not one of two.
- `settings.tsx` — Not a tab; reached only from `profile.tsx`'s "Settings" action. Four sections (see Core Flow "Association Settings" for full detail) — Association Information (Name/Acronym/Email/Contact Number/Address, password required only when Email or Contact Number changes), Notifications (8 inert local preference toggles), Privacy & Security (Change Password + static security/session info), Terms & Policies (`ASSOCIATION_TERMS`/`PRIVACY_POLICY` viewers). No Delete/Deactivate action anywhere on this screen.

**Brand colors:** primary `#1a4a52` (dark teal), accent `#a8d832` (yellow-green)

---

# Admin Panel

**SPA** at `admin_panel/transync-admin/`. Auth guard: `localStorage.getItem('admin')`.

| Route | Page | API calls |
|-------|------|-----------|
| `/` | Login.jsx | `POST /api/auth/login.php` |
| `/overview` | Overview.jsx | `GET /api/system/overview.php` |
| `/user-management` | UserManagement.jsx | `GET /api/system/users.php` + DELETE |
| `/verify-users` | VerifyUsers.jsx | `GET /api/auth/verify.php?type=X` + `POST /api/auth/verify.php` |
| `/terminal-management` | TerminalManagement.jsx | `GET /api/system/terminals.php` |
| `/system-wallet` | SystemWallet.jsx | `GET /api/system/system_wallet.php` + `GET`/`POST /api/system/subscription_rate.php` |

Sidebar `NAV` array is duplicated per-page (no shared Sidebar component) — adding a nav entry means editing every page file's `NAV` array + icon-switch block, not just one place.

Vite dev proxy: `vite.config.js` proxies `/api` → `http://localhost/TranSync-App/transync-backend/api/`

---

# Critical Insights

## Login edge cases
- Rejected commuters can still log in (status `inactive` + `rejected_at` set) but their `category` is forced to `default` and `is_verified` to `0` in the response.
- `operator` table uses `status = active` as the initial state after registration (verified by admin after), unlike `commuters` which also use `active` but `is_verified = 0`.
- Driver login unconditionally requires `vehicle_id` (403 if missing) — `operator_id` is always set at account creation (operator-managed, no self-registration), so there's no separate "no operator yet" state to special-case anymore.

## Queue atomicity
- `join_queue.php` always nukes prior `waiting` rows for that driver before inserting — prevents duplicate queue entries if the app retries.
- Queue position = COUNT of `waiting` rows with smaller `id`, not by timestamp. IDs are auto-increment and effectively chronological.
- Dispatched drivers do **not** block queue; only `waiting` rows count for position.

## Fare & wallet
- `end_ride.php` reads `discount_type` from the ride record (set at boarding), never from the current commuter state. This prevents fare manipulation mid-ride.
- Wallet is never a hard blocker for rides: if balance < fare, fare is capped at balance.
- All money operations use `$conn->begin_transaction()` / `commit()` / `rollback()`.

## Route waypoints
- Stored as JSON in `routes.waypoints` (not in `route_waypoints` table).
- Both `{lat, lng}` and `{latitude, longitude}` key formats appear in waypoints — `route_planner.php` normalizes via `wpLat()` / `wpLng()` helpers.

## File uploads
- Stored under `transync-backend/uploads/{commuters|operators}/`
- DB stores relative path: `uploads/commuters/filename.jpg`
- `verify.php` prepends `http://localhost/TranSync-App/transync-backend/` when returning document URLs to the admin panel

## Auth security
- A real Bearer-token session system exists (`config/auth.php`: `createSession()`/`requireAuth()`/`destroySession()`, backed by a `sessions` table) and `login_mobile.php` does issue a token — but almost no endpoint calls `requireAuth()`, and the mobile client never stores or sends the token (only `user` JSON + `role` go into AsyncStorage). Treat the app as if there were no session tokens: nearly all endpoints trust `driver_id`/`commuter_id`/etc. straight from the request body/query, no server-side session validation in practice.
- CORS: `Access-Control-Allow-Origin: *` on all mobile endpoints.
- Passwords: `password_hash($password, PASSWORD_BCRYPT)` + `password_verify`.

## PHP/MySQL clock mismatch (landmine)
- This server's `php.ini` has `date.timezone = Europe/Berlin`, but MySQL's `SYSTEM` timezone matches the actual machine clock (Philippines-ish). They disagree by several hours.
- **Never compare PHP's `time()`/`date()` (no args) against a MySQL `DATETIME` string parsed with `strtotime()`** — the naive string gets interpreted under the wrong offset, producing a multi-hour skew. This bit the UV Express slot QR gate during development (see `board_reservation.php`).
- Fix pattern (already used in `get_active_sessions.php` and now `board_reservation.php`/`create_reservation.php`/`create_slot.php`/`get_slot_passengers.php`): fetch `SELECT NOW() AS now_ts` from the DB and run **both** sides of the comparison through `strtotime()` — the offset is then applied consistently to both and cancels out. Pure formatting of a single DB string (no comparison against "now") is safe either way.

## UV Express dispatch queue (route-scoped)
- `dispatch_queue` (originally Jeepney-only) now has a nullable `route_id` column. Jeepney rows keep `route_id = NULL` and behave exactly as before — many jeepneys can be `dispatched` simultaneously, unenforced FIFO.
- UV Express rows always set `route_id`. For route-scoped rows, `start_trip.php` enforces **one `dispatched` driver per `(operator_id, route_id)` at a time** and requires the caller to be first in the `waiting` line — because becoming "dispatched" now grants authority to scan and get paid for that route's scheduled UV slot bookings (`board_reservation.php` resolves boarding authorization via the currently-dispatched driver for the slot's route, not a fixed driver assigned at booking time). Do not extend this enforcement to unscoped (Jeepney) rows.
- **Operator-facing manual queue advance** (`operators/get_uv_queue_status.php` GET, `operators/advance_uv_queue.php` POST — both `{operator_id, route_id}`) layers directly on top of these same `dispatch_queue` rows: it's an operator-triggered version of the same `dispatched→completed` / `waiting→dispatched` transition a driver can already self-trigger via `start_trip.php`/`leave_queue.php`, reusing the identical FIFO (`MIN(id)` of `waiting`). Because both paths write to the same rows, they can never conflict destructively — this doesn't replace or gate driver self-service, it's purely an additional control. Mobile: `(operator)/trips.tsx`'s UV Express tab shows a "Route Queue" panel per active route (current dispatched driver + waiting list) with an "Advance to Next Vehicle" button. Scoped to UV Express only — Jeepney has no booking/reservation concept for "new bookings auto-assign to the active vehicle" to attach to, so this was deliberately not extended there.

---

# Known Issues / Risks

1. **Token auth exists but is unused** — a `sessions` table + `requireAuth()` helper exist, but the mobile client doesn't send tokens and almost no endpoint requires them. Any client that knows a valid user ID can call any endpoint on their behalf.
2. **Wide-open CORS** — Acceptable for local dev; must be restricted in production.
3. **Nominatim dependency** — `route_planner.php` makes a live HTTP call to OSM Nominatim with 8s timeout. Network failures silently return no geocoding result.
4. **`nearby_drivers.php` Jeepney-only** — UV Express and Bus operators have no nearby-driver discovery endpoint.
5. **No pagination** — `wallet.php` returns last 20 transactions hardcoded; `users.php` and others return all rows.
6. **Google Maps API key** — In `app.json` (visible in source). Should be env-gated.
7. **Legacy per-driver association tables still present** — `association_applications`/`association_memberships` (and their endpoints `applications/submit_application.php`, `review_application.php`, `pending_applications.php`, `associations/kick_member.php`, `leave_association.php`) are dormant, superseded by the operator-level org hierarchy, but not deleted. Safe to remove once confirmed nothing references them.
8. **On-hold gating is UI-only** — `isOperational` (verified + approved association) gates operator screens client-side; the underlying endpoints (`drivers.php` POST, `vehicles.php` POST, etc.) don't independently re-check association approval, consistent with this app's general lack of server-side session enforcement (see Auth security).
9. **UV Express "capacity includes the driver" isn't auto-subtracted anywhere** — `vehicles.capacity` for a UV Express vehicle stores the total including the driver's seat (9–18), but nothing currently computes `capacity - 1` for passenger-facing counts; that's left to whatever UI needs it.
10. **Jeepney's digital fare/wallet mechanism is now fully dormant** — Core Flow 4/5's QR-boarding + wallet fare deduction is dead (driver no longer displays a QR; Jeepney payment is traditional/cash, off-app). `verify_qr_board.php`, `request_dropoff.php`, `end_ride.php`, `active_ride.php`, `latest_ride.php`, and `(main)/scan.tsx` are all left in place unreachable. If this ever needs reviving, restoring the QR display in `driver-map.tsx` is the only missing piece — the backend was left fully intact. **One real casualty of this dormancy was found and fixed 2026-08-10**: `drivers/driver_summary.php` (the driver home screen's "Today's Summary" stat) still unconditionally queried `jeepney_rides` for every driver regardless of vehicle type — a table nothing writes to anymore for Jeepney, and one Bus/UV Express drivers' rows were never in to begin with — so the stat silently showed 0 trips/₱0 earnings for every driver of every type, with no error surfaced anywhere. First fix branched by vehicle type (Jeepney → completed `dispatch_queue` cycles today; Bus/UV Express → today's driver `wallet_transactions` credits) — but this **undercounted Bus/UV Express trips**: the Dispatch tab's Join Queue → Start Trip → End Trip flow is the exact same shared `dispatch_queue` mechanism for all three vehicle types (see the UV Express dispatch queue Critical Insight), so a Bus/UV driver who completed that cycle with no passenger ever boarded correctly still expects "Trips" to reflect it, not stay at 0. **Corrected same day**: `trip_count` now counts completed `dispatch_queue` cycles for every vehicle type uniformly; `total_earnings` stays Bus/UV-Express-only, sourced from `wallet_transactions` credits (`reference_type='booking'`, the same ledger `creditRemittanceSplit()` already writes and the Wallet tab already reads) — Jeepney has no per-ride app earnings by design. Verified live: a scratch Bus driver running a full queue cycle with zero passengers boarded correctly shows `trip_count:1, total_earnings:0`; adding a booking-credit wallet_transactions row alongside it correctly shows `total_earnings` reflecting just that credit. The "Km Today" stat was removed from `(driver)/dashboard.tsx` entirely rather than fixed — no per-trip distance is tracked anywhere in this schema for any vehicle type (it only ever existed on the now-dead `jeepney_rides.distance_km`), so there was no real data to source it from without a new distance-tracking feature, out of scope for this fix. Anyone reviving Jeepney's digital fare flow should re-check whether `driver_summary.php`'s Jeepney branch still wants distance-tracking added at that point.
11. **No admin approval UI for driver withdrawal requests** — `drivers/driver_withdraw.php` still debits the driver's wallet immediately and inserts a `pending` row, but nothing in the admin panel lists or resolves them yet; they stay `pending` until resolved directly in the DB. **The operator side no longer has this gap** — `wallet/operator_withdraw.php` was rewritten 2026-09-20 to complete instantly (`status='paid'` on insert, no approval step at all, see Do Not Break rule #76) — the two flows are now deliberately asymmetric, not mirrors of each other.

---

# Do Not Break (Critical Rules)

1. **Fare formula**: ₱13 base, 4km included, ₱1.80/km after, 20% discount for verified student/disabled/elderly, round to nearest ₱0.25.
2. **Queue FIFO by id**: position counting uses `id <` comparison, not timestamps.
3. **Ghost-row cleanup in join_queue**: the `UPDATE … status = completed` before INSERT must stay — prevents duplicate waiting entries.
4. **Wallet transaction atomicity**: all money moves must be inside `begin_transaction / commit / rollback`.
5. **Login sequence order** (commuter → driver → operator → association): changing order could misroute users with cross-table email conflicts.
6. **48-hour resubmission cooldown**: enforced in `resubmit.php` using `rejected_at` timestamp diff.
7. **discount_type captured at boarding**: `verify_qr_board.php` sets it on the ride row; `end_ride.php` reads from ride, not from commuter state.
8. **Pending commuter filter**: `rejected_at IS NULL` is required — rejected commuters with `is_verified=0` must not appear in the pending queue.
9. **Wallet never blocks ride**: `end_ride.php` caps fare at available balance instead of rejecting.
10. **Admin PrivateRoute**: guard reads `localStorage.getItem('admin')` — change the key name and all admin sessions break.
11. **Booking advance window**: both Bus and UV Express (scheduled slots) require 24h+ notice, enforced in `create_reservation.php` — constants live in `config/policy.php` (`ADVANCE_BOOKING_HOURS`), don't hardcode them elsewhere.
12. **Refund is a two-tier percentage, computed and settled by exactly one endpoint** (rewritten 2026-08-11, simplified 2026-08-12 — dropped the 0% tier and the post-booking grace period; supersedes the old two-endpoint binary-cutoff design described in earlier revisions of this rule): `reservations/request_refund.php` is the sole commuter-facing cancellation endpoint, computing the outcome via `config/refund.php`'s `computeRefundOutcome()` against `REFUND_TIERS` (`config/policy.php`) — 100% at or beyond the vehicle type's threshold (Bus 72h, UV Express 24h), 50% for anything short of it, no matter how close to departure. There is no grace period tied to how soon after booking the cancellation happens, and no cancellation is ever refused a refund entirely. It both credits whatever is refunded to the wallet *and* forfeits the other half via the normal remittance split, in the same transaction — a single cancellation is always partly "money comes back," partly "money is gone." `reservations/cancel_late.php` is deprecated in place (frozen at an earlier binary policy, no longer called). Commuter self-service rebooking still doesn't exist — see rule 29.
13. **Student-discount dedup**: `create_reservation.php` blocks a second discounted booking by the same commuter on the same `trip_id`/`uv_session_id`/`uv_slot_id` — forces `discount_type='default'` on the repeat booking instead of rejecting it outright.
14. **Escrow payment_status lifecycle**: `reservations.payment_status` only moves `held → released` (boarding), `held → refunded` (refund — `'refunded'` always covers a partial (50%) outcome as well as a full one now, since there's no 0% tier; check `refund_pct`/`refund_amount` for the actual amount, never assume `'refunded'` means "in full"), or `held → forfeited` (no-show sweep — the only source of a 0%-to-commuter outcome left in the system, since cancellation itself never drops below 50%). Never set it back to `held`.
15. **Driver accounts are operator-created only**: `drivers/drivers.php` POST, always with `operator_id` set at creation. Never add a public driver self-registration path or a driver-initiated "apply to operator" flow — that was built and deliberately reverted (migrations 011→017); re-introducing it needs the same conversation, not just a code change. Operator→association applies with `operator_association_applications` stays (fleet-level, no driver/vehicle subject) — never let a driver apply directly to an association either.
16. **Association membership is transitive and per-vehicle-type, never stored per-driver or on a single operator column**: always derive via `operator_association_applications WHERE operator_id=? AND vehicle_type=? AND status='approved'` (scoped to the driver's own vehicle's type, or whichever vehicle type an endpoint is acting on) — never read/write `drivers.association_id` (legacy, unsynced) or `operators.association_id` (deprecated, an operator can hold multiple simultaneous memberships now so one FK can't represent that).
17. **`depart` must never immediately expire reservations**: that logic moved to the grace-period sweep. Only `cancel` (refund) and `complete` (forfeit) resolve lingering `reserved` bookings synchronously.
18. **QR grace period math must use the DB clock**: `board_reservation.php`/`sweep_departed_trips.php`/`get_reservation_status.php`/`preview_ticket.php` all compare `departure_time + QR_GRACE_MINUTES[vehicle_category]` against `SELECT NOW()`, never PHP `time()` — same timezone landmine as everywhere else in this file. `QR_GRACE_MINUTES` is a per-vehicle-type map (`config/policy.php`), not a flat number (Bus 20 min, UV Express 30 min) — every caller must key it off the reservation's own `vehicle_category`, never assume one shared value.
19. **Remittance percentages apply at the moment of the money event, not at booking**: `creditRemittanceSplit()` (`config/remittance.php`) computes the system/operator/driver split from `remittance_rates` at the instant it's called (boarding scan, late cancellation, or no-show sweep) and stores the amounts on the reservation — don't recompute from current rates later, historical bookings must keep the split that was actually in effect.
20. **Jeepney quota tier never overwrites an operator's customization**: `end_trip.php` only auto-inserts into `driver_quotas` when no row exists yet for that driver.
21. **Seat selection is Bus-only**: never add a `seat_label` requirement to UV bookings — UV slots have no fixed `vehicle_id`, there's no seat map to enforce against.
22. **Vehicle `capacity` validation is per vehicle type, not universal**: Jeepney stays an even number 12–36 (`jeepney_quota_tiers` ranges are keyed off it — widening this means adding a matching tier row too, see migration 016). UV Express is 9–18, any integer (includes the driver's seat, no even-only rule). Bus takes no capacity at registration at all — it's set later by `seed_seats.php`. Don't collapse these back into one shared rule.
23. **No manual "cash out" step for operators**: confirming a quota submission (`review_submission.php`) must credit `operators.wallet_balance` in the same transaction as the status change. Don't reintroduce a separate cash-out action — the operator UI intentionally has only one money-out action (Withdraw).
24. **Bus classes and fares are association-owned, never operator-writable**: `bus_service_types` (via `associations/service_types.php`) and `fare_matrix` (via `set_service_fare.php`) both require `association_id` and validate ownership against it. Never add an `operator_id`-authorized write path to either — an operator's only Bus-side actions are picking a class for its vehicle and building that vehicle's seat layout (`seed_seats.php`).
25. **Bus routes can only be association-owned**: `save_route.php` and the legacy `routes.php` both reject creating a `vehicle_type='Bus'` route under `operator_id`. `create_trip.php`'s ownership check must keep accepting association-owned routes (via the scheduling operator's own *Bus-approved* `operator_association_applications` row, not the deprecated `operators.association_id`), not just operator-owned ones, or Bus trip scheduling breaks entirely for every operator that isn't grandfathered onto a legacy operator-owned route.
40. **A seat with an active booking (`boarding_status IN ('reserved','boarded')`) can never be removed or renamed**, whether through `vehicles/save_seat_layout.php`'s diff-and-upsert or `vehicles/apply_seat_template.php`'s template copy — both check across *all* of the vehicle's trips (a layout is a property of the vehicle, not one trip) and reject the whole operation (returning `locked_seats`) if any submitted layout is missing a booked label. Repositioning (row/col) or retyping a booked seat while keeping its label is fine — the booking is tied to the label, not position.
41. **Bus trip assignment has no vehicle/driver scheduling-conflict check of any kind — deliberately** (rewritten 2026-08-03, twice in the same day: first from a same-`DATE()` block to a time-overlap + rest-period check, then that check was explicitly removed entirely on request). `bus_trips/assign_trip.php` and `reassign_trip.php` never reject an assignment for overlapping schedules, back-to-back trips, or insufficient turnaround time — the operator has full control and the system never blocks on this. `bus_trips/create_trip.php` (legacy/dormant, unused by any current mobile screen) still has its own old same-`DATE()` double-booking check from before this rewrite — left as-is since nothing calls it, not worth touching. Don't reintroduce any form of vehicle/driver conflict blocking (time-overlap, rest-period, or same-date) into `assign_trip.php`/`reassign_trip.php` without the same conversation that removed it — this was tried (a flat 2h-duration + 8h-rest assumption, since no real trip-duration/arrival-time data exists anywhere in this schema) and explicitly reverted.
